- **envelope**: Кожна операція повертає конверт однакової форми. Головне в ньому — **не `data`**. У `data` лежить те, що знайшлось; у попередженнях — те, чи можна цьому вірити. Плутати їх дорого: саме тут різниця між «немає» і «не шукали». ([SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka](https://github.com/SERGIUSH-UA/nyshporka/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/envelope.md) · ★20)
- **envelope-analyst**: Building envelope analysis specialist for EnergyPlus models. Expert in constructions, materials, fenestration, thermal properties, and ASHRAE 90.1 envelope requirements. Use for reviewing and optimizing the building envelope. ([idfkit/idfkit-plugin](https://github.com/idfkit/idfkit-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/envelope-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **envelope-engine**: Implements Abaqo's pure domain logic - envelopes, monthly allocation, month close (surplus and shortfall rules), goals, sub-categories and shared expenses. - **eos-implementer**: Implementer fallback: writes code and normal tests for one bounded slice and runs the repository-owned verification. Uses the current session model. - **eos-mechanics**: Implement or investigate one authorized Eight Months of Silence mechanics ticket with minimal changes, existing Yumiwa services and real regression evidence. - **eos-qa**: Independently review one Eight Months of Silence ticket for impactful regressions, story acceptance and truthful verification evidence. ([ZxWyvern/Eight-Month-Of-Silence](https://github.com/ZxWyvern/Eight-Month-Of-Silence/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/eos-qa.md) · 2 copies)
- **eos-researcher**: Research agent for EOS. Use for ICP intelligence gathering, market signal discovery, competitor analysis, and any research task requiring web search. - **eos-researcher**: Answers a question about this codebase with evidence — starts from what earlier sessions learned (EOS notes and runs), proves it from the real source with path:line, and records what a future scan could not re-derive. - **eos-reviewer**: Fresh reviewer fallback: checks the full diff and evidence at one exact final commit. It is one reviewer, not a panel. ([acartag7/engineering-os](https://github.com/acartag7/engineering-os/blob/HEAD/plugins/engineering-os/agents/eos-reviewer.md))
- **eos-simplifier**: Code simplification agent. Use after any implementation is complete. Reviews for reuse opportunities, quality issues, and efficiency. - **eos-spec-critic**: Fresh critic fallback: finds missing decisions, unsafe silences, slice problems, and weak verification before implementation. ([acartag7/engineering-os](https://github.com/acartag7/engineering-os/blob/HEAD/plugins/engineering-os/agents/eos-spec-critic.md))
- **eos-story**: Ground one Eight Months of Silence ticket in canonical chapter beats, evidence, player actions and acceptance criteria; preserve unresolved narrative decisions. - **eos-verifier**: Verification agent. Use after any implementation to verify correctness. Runs imports, checks for errors, validates expected behavior. - **eos-workflow**: Execute source-grounded development tasks for Eight Months of Silence using its AGENTS contract, Yumiwa code map, nine workflow phases, and evidence gates. - **ep-kit-reviewer**: Use for an independent skeptical review of a Draft EP or an implementation-status claim after the primary agent has finished its own review. ([foobarto/strata](https://github.com/foobarto/strata/blob/HEAD/agents/ep-kit-reviewer.md) · ★1)

- **ep-planner**: Rough implementation plan agent in the expert panel. Given the final recommendation, produces 3-8 high-level steps with rough timing and dependencies. Not a detailed implementation plan. ([phyr97/expert-panel](https://github.com/phyr97/expert-panel/blob/HEAD/agents/ep-planner.md))
- **ep-synthesizer**: Synthesizer in the expert panel. Reads all proposer outputs and the critique; produces the final recommendation with explicit trade-offs and rejected alternatives. Supports one-shot synthesis (default) and pairwise Elo tournament (when --elo is passed). ([phyr97/expert-panel](https://github.com/phyr97/expert-panel/blob/HEAD/agents/ep-synthesizer.md))
- **ep0ch-auditor**: Read-only review of the ep0ch codebase or of a merged wave through one named lens (architecture and reuse, correctness, safety, tests that earn their place, docs and skills against the code, daily-driver interaction). Finds and ranks problems with file:line evidence, fixes nothing, writes one findings note in an outline. Use for push reviews, wave reviews and audits. ([float-ritual-stack/ep0ch](https://github.com/float-ritual-stack/ep0ch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ep0ch-auditor.md))
- **ep0ch-builder**: Builds one ep0ch experience ("Evan opens X and can do Y", with every ticket it needs) end to end in its own worktree - code, tests, docs, kitchen-sink demo, a walk of the sentence in a real door - opens one PR and hands loki, the deployer, its number and sha. Use for implementation work in this repository; the brief names the sentence, the ticket(s) and anything the rules here don't cover. ([float-ritual-stack/ep0ch](https://github.com/float-ritual-stack/ep0ch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ep0ch-builder.md))
- **ep0ch-recon**: Read-only research in the ep0ch repository and its outlines - how something works today, where a concept lives, what a change would touch, what prior art exists. Changes no code; writes its findings as one note in an outline and returns the conclusion. Use before planning a feature or ticket, or to answer "how does X work?". ([float-ritual-stack/ep0ch](https://github.com/float-ritual-stack/ep0ch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ep0ch-recon.md))
- **ep0ch-verifier**: Takes an old or disputed ep0ch ticket (a bug report, or work said to be done) and checks it against today's main on a scratch outline - reproduces the bug or exercises the feature, then either reports it closable with evidence or fixes it test-first through a PR. Use for backlog sweeps, validate-lane sweeps and "is this still broken?". ([float-ritual-stack/ep0ch](https://github.com/float-ritual-stack/ep0ch/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ep0ch-verifier.md))
- **epc-model-builder**: Build event-driven process chain models for ME/CFS biological processes with explicit event triggers, temporal annotations, and branching probabilities. ([yannickloth/health-me-cfs](https://github.com/yannickloth/health-me-cfs/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/epc-model-builder.md))
- **ephodos**: The derive lane of lean-dir's pipeline — Ephodos, the deriver. Launch it for a milestone's opening (the reframe — re-grokking, prior-art gate, invariant question — the plan and the claim list), for a derivation job on a fixed claim list, for a re-derivation after a counterexample or a statement defect, or for the milestone's record job (the derivation file, the stubs and the drafts of the front page), which makes the milestone Derived. Its derived statements go, through Keryx, to de Bruijn's planner. Not for Lean (debruijn), searching (searcher), or choosing what to work on next (poros). ([kooshi/hadwiger-nelson](https://github.com/kooshi/hadwiger-nelson/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ephodos.md) · ★2)
- **ephodos**: The derive lane of this project's pipeline — Ephodos, the deriver. Launch it for a milestone's opening (the reframe — re-grokking, prior-art gate, invariant question — the plan and the claim list), for a derivation job on a fixed claim list, for a re-derivation after a counterexample or a statement defect, or for the milestone's record job (the derivation file, the stubs and the drafts of the front page), which makes the milestone Derived. Its derived statements go, through Keryx, to de Bruijn's planner. Not for Lean (debruijn), searching (searcher), or choosing what to work on next (poros). ([kooshi/agentic-mathematics](https://github.com/kooshi/agentic-mathematics/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ephodos.md) · ★1)
- **ephodos-verifier**: A blind second mind — Ephodos in its verification form. NOT in the standard workflow since Workflow 3.1 (the kernel is the second check); launched only on Kooshi's word. It derives each statement of a fixed claim list by its own route, attacks the statement itself, commits its verdicts, and only then reads the first derivation and writes the divergence table. Never handed a verdict, a sketch or a route. ([kooshi/hadwiger-nelson](https://github.com/kooshi/hadwiger-nelson/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ephodos-verifier.md) · ★2)
- **ephodos-verifier**: A blind second mind — Ephodos in its verification form. NOT in the standard workflow (the kernel is the second check); launched only on the principal's word. - **epic-controller**: An epic controller is a Claude session that owns one epic (`E&lt;n&gt;`) end to end. Started with ./start-claude.sh --epic N (or SESSION_EPIC=N). ([kube-dojo/kube-dojo.github.io](https://github.com/kube-dojo/kube-dojo.github.io/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/epic-driver.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)

- **epic-exit**: Epic-boundary check. Every ticket can pass while the epic still fails to deliver what SPEC 14.3 says it should. Verifies the epic's stated exit criterion against the running system. Always run before an epic merges to main. ([toddawhittaker/pulse-surveys](https://github.com/toddawhittaker/pulse-surveys/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/epic-exit.md))
- **epic-explorer**: Reconnaissance agent for Epic Constraints authoring — surfaces decomposition candidates, refactor opportunities (foundational/opportunistic/deferred bins), and convention conflicts in the active repo's relevant subtree at epic altitude ([solventgrun/armature](https://github.com/solventgrun/armature/blob/HEAD/plugins/epic-design/agents/epic-explorer.md))
- **epic-framer**: Cadre un EPIC de Waterfall jusqu'à ses issues : détaille les US avec l'utilisateur, écrit la conception, établit le plan de lots, en s'arrêtant pour validation après chacune de ces trois étapes, puis ouvre les issues. À utiliser pour préparer un EPIC `à planifier` — souvent le suivant, pendant que le précédent se livre. Ne tire pas la branche de l'EPIC et n'écrit aucun code. Lancé « à blanc », rend la conception et le plan de lots qu'il proposerait, sans rien publier. ([waterfall-project/wf-project](https://github.com/waterfall-project/wf-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/epic-framer.md) · ★2)
- **epic-graphs**: This project's only source for multi-role epic construction. A graph is guidance, not board data: `taskd` never reads it, nothing is stored or inherited. The recipe that instantiates a graph is the `meerkat` skill's `references/scenarios/epic.md`. ([tanyu216/meerkat-taskpanel](https://github.com/tanyu216/meerkat-taskpanel/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/epic-graphs.md) · ★1)
- **epic-group-worker**: Runs one lane of an epic — a sequential queue of tickets that owns a file territory no other lane touches. Used by the implement-epic skill, one instance per group, all spawned concurrently in isolated worktrees. It plans, implements, reviews and commits each ticket in its queue and pushes the branch chain; it never opens a PR and never merges to main. ([tareq89/biddaloy](https://github.com/tareq89/biddaloy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/epic-group-worker.md))

- **epic-implementer**: Implements a full CRM epic OR a single ticket from the JIRA backlog. For a full epic pass the epic key e.g. "implement EPIC-PLAT" or "implement EPIC-LEAD".
